li < links.length; li++) {\n        var row = links[li]\n        var igKey = 'prosperity_' + String(row.ig).toLowerCase()\n        var igVal = q[igKey]\n        if (igVal == null) { igVal = igProsperityDefault(row.ig) }\n        var signal = Number(igVal)\n        if (isNaN(signal)) { signal = igProsperityDefault(row.ig) }\n        var sourcePop = PROSPERITY_IG_EFFECTIVE_POP[row.ig]\n        if (sourcePop == null) { sourcePop = 20 }\n        var blocks = prosperityBlocksInDistrict(state, row.district)\n        var toStrata = row.toStrata || {}\n\n        for (var bi = 0; bi < blocks.length; bi++) {\n            var block = blocks[bi]\n            var sid = block.demographic.id\n            if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(toStrata, sid)) {\n                continue\n            }\n            var coeff = Number(toStrata[sid])\n            if (isNaN(coeff) || coeff === 0) { continue }\n            var weight = prosperityTransferMultiplier(sourcePop, block.population)\n            var transfer = coeff * signal * weight\n            if (transfer === 0) { continue }\n            blockDeltas[block.id] = (blockDeltas[block.id] || 0) + transfer\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nfunction applyDocksTradeProsperityLinks(state, q, blockDeltas) {\n    var spec = Q.prosperityDocksTrade || PROSPERITY_DOCKS_TRADE\n    var excess = economyQualityExcess(q, spec.quality)\n    if (excess === 0) { return }\n    var sourcePop = prosperityDistrictTotalPop(state, spec.district)\n    if (sourcePop <= 0) { return }\n    var blocks = prosperityBlocksInDistrict(state, spec.district)\n    var strataCoeffs = spec.strata || {}\n\n    for (var bi = 0; bi < blocks.length; bi++) {\n        var block = blocks[bi]\n        var sid = block.demographic.id\n        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(strataCoeffs, sid)) {\n            continue\n        }\n        var strataMult = Number(strataCoeffs[sid])\n        if (isNaN(strataMult) || strataMult === 0) { continue }\n        var coeff = Number(spec.coefficient) * strataMult\n        if (isNaN(coeff) || coeff === 0) { continue }\n        var weight = prosperityTransferMultiplier(sourcePop, block.population)\n        var transfer = coeff * excess * weight\n        if (transfer === 0) { continue }\n        blockDeltas[block.id] = (blockDeltas[block.id] || 0) + transfer\n    }\n}\n\nfunction applyPittsCrimeProsperityLinks(state, q, blockDeltas) {\n    var spec = Q.prosperityPittsCrime || PROSPERITY_PITTS_CRIME\n    var excess = economyQualityExcess(q, spec.quality)\n    if (excess === 0) { return }\n    var sourcePop = prosperityDistrictTotalPop(state, spec.district)\n    if (sourcePop <= 0) { return }\n    var blocks = prosperityBlocksInDistrict(state, spec.district)\n    var strataCoeffs = spec.strata || {}\n    var baseCoeff = Number(spec.coefficient)\n    if (isNaN(baseCoeff)) { baseCoeff = 0 }\n\n    for (var bi = 0; bi < blocks.length; bi++) {\n        var block = blocks[bi]\n        var sid = block.demographic.id\n        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(strataCoeffs, sid)) { continue }\n        var strataMult = Number(strataCoeffs[sid])\n        if (isNaN(strataMult) || strataMult === 0) { continue }\n        var coeff = baseCoeff * strataMult\n        var weight = prosperityTransferMultiplier(sourcePop, block.population)\n        var transfer = coeff * excess * weight\n        if (transfer === 0) { continue }\n        blockDeltas[block.id] = (blockDeltas[block.id] || 0) + transfer\n    }\n}\n\n/**\n * One-shot citywide prosperity grants per stratum via Q.<stratum>_bump (e.g. coop_bump).\n * Adds bump to every voter block in that class, clamps, syncs Q, then sets the key to 0.\n * Cards:  on-arrival: coop_bump = 0.05\n * Same month: call Q.applyStratumProsperityBumps() in on-arrival after setting bump.\n * Otherwise applied at the start of monthly_tick_phase1 (before connection bleed).\n */\nfunction applyStratumProsperityBumps() {\n    var state = Q.electionV2\n    if (!state || !state.blocks) { return false }\n\n    var strata = Q.stratum_labels || [\n        'rich', 'first', 'second', 'coop', 'drone', 'alien', 'deeper', 'third_wave',\n    ]\n    var changed = false\n\n    for (var s = 0; s < strata.length; s++) {\n        var sid = strata[s]\n        var key = sid + '_bump'\n        var raw = Q[key]\n        if (raw == null || raw === 0) {\n            Q[key] = 0\n            continue\n        }\n        var bump = Number(raw)\n        Q[key] = 0\n        if (isNaN(bump) || bump === 0) { continue }\n\n        for (var i = 0; i < state.blocks.length; i++) {\n            var block = state.blocks[i]\n            if (block.demographic.id !== sid) { continue }\n            block.prosperity = clampProsperity(\n                (block.prosperity ?? PROSPERITY_BLOCK_BASELINE) + bump\n            )\n        }\n        changed = true\n    }\n\n    if (changed && typeof Q.qified_block_prosperity === 'function') {\n        Q.qified_block_prosperity()\n    }\n    return changed\n}\n\nfunction applyProsperityConnections() {\n    var state = Q.electionV2\n    if (!state || !state.blocks || !Q.prosperityConnectionCatalog) { return }\n\n    initEconomyProsperity()\n\n    var blockDeltas = {}\n    var igDeltas = {}\n    var catalog = Q.prosperityConnectionCatalog\n\n    for (var i = 0; i < catalog.length; i++) {\n        catalog[i].accumulate(state, Q, blockDeltas, igDeltas)\n    }\n\n    applyDistrictIntraProsperityMesh(state, blockDeltas)\n    applyIgHomeDistrictProsperityLinks(state, Q, blockDeltas)\n    applyDocksTradeProsperityLinks(state, Q, blockDeltas)\n    applyPittsCrimeProsperityLinks(state, Q, blockDeltas)\n\n    for (var b = 0; b < state.blocks.length; b++) {\n        var block = state.blocks[b]\n        var delta = blockDeltas[block.id]\n        if (delta) {\n            block.prosperity = clampProsperity(\n                (block.prosperity ?? PROSPERITY_BLOCK_BASELINE) + delta\n            )\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (var igKey in igDeltas) {\n        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(igDeltas, igKey)) { continue }\n        var igVal = Q[igKey]\n        if (igVal == null) {\n            igVal = igProsperityDefault(igIdFromProsperityKey(igKey))\n        }\n        Q[igKey] = clampProsperity(igVal + igDeltas[igKey])\n    }\n\n    if (typeof Q.qified_block_prosperity === 'function') {\n        Q.qified_block_prosperity()\n    }\n}\n\nQ.ProsperityConnectionModifier = ProsperityConnectionModifier\n\n// Legacy rows (decay, card stacks). District mesh + home IG + Docks/Pitts: constants above.\nQ.prosperityConnectionCatalog = [\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_rich', 'stratum', 'rich', 'stratum', 'rich', -0.05, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_first', 'stratum', 'first', 'stratum', 'first', -0.05, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_second', 'stratum', 'second', 'stratum', 'second', -0.105, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_coop', 'stratum', 'coop', 'stratum', 'coop', -0.25, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_drone', 'stratum', 'drone', 'stratum', 'drone', -0.02, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_alien', 'stratum', 'alien', 'stratum', 'alien', -0.02, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_deeper', 'stratum', 'deeper', 'stratum', 'deeper', -0.02, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_third_wave', 'stratum', 'third_wave', 'stratum', 'third_wave', -0.05, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_soylent', 'ig', 'Soylent', 'ig', 'Soylent', -0.30, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_magrail', 'ig', 'Magrail', 'ig', 'Magrail', -0.30, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_brazos', 'ig', 'Brazos', 'ig', 'Brazos', -0.30, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_families', 'ig', 'Families', 'ig', 'Families', -0.02, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_edges', 'ig', 'Edges', 'ig', 'Edges', -0.02, { self_feedback: true }),\n    new ProsperityConnectionModifier('decay_sharks', 'ig', 'Sharks', 'ig', 'Sharks', -0.02, { self_feedback: true }),\n]\n\nQ.prosperityIntraDistrictMeshDefault = PROSPERITY_INTRA_DISTRICT_DEFAULT\nQ.prosperityIgHomeDistrictLinks = PROSPERITY_IG_HOME_DISTRICT_LINKS\nQ.prosperityDocksTrade = PROSPERITY_DOCKS_TRADE\nQ.prosperityPittsCrime = PROSPERITY_PITTS_CRIME\nQ.prosperityDistrictIds = PROSPERITY_DISTRICT_IDS\nQ.prosperityIgEffectivePop = PROSPERITY_IG_EFFECTIVE_POP\nQ.prosperityBlockBaseline = PROSPERITY_BLOCK_BASELINE\nQ.prosperityIgStart = PROSPERITY_IG_START\nQ.prosperityClampMin = PROSPERITY_CLAMP_MIN\nQ.prosperityClampMax = PROSPERITY_CLAMP_MAX\n\n/** IG prosperity defaults (root @start); block baseline lives in election_runtime. */\nfunction initEconomyProsperity() {\n    if (Q.prosperity_soylent == null) {\n        Q.prosperity_soylent = PROSPERITY_IG_START.Soylent\n    }\n    if (Q.prosperity_magrail == null) {\n        Q.prosperity_magrail = PROSPERITY_IG_START.Magrail\n    }\n    if (Q.prosperity_brazos == null) {\n        Q.prosperity_brazos = PROSPERITY_IG_START.Brazos\n    }\n    if (Q.prosperity_families == null) {\n        Q.prosperity_families = PROSPERITY_IG_START.Families\n    }\n    if (Q.prosperity_edges == null) {\n        Q.prosperity_edges = PROSPERITY_IG_START.Edges\n    }\n    if (Q.prosperity_sharks == null) {\n        Q.prosperity_sharks = PROSPERITY_IG_START.Sharks\n    }\n}\n\n/** Map IG prosperity → Status tickers (scaled so index 10 ≈ opening quotes). */\nfunction syncProsperityDisplays() {\n    initEconomyProsperity()\n    var scale = PROSPERITY_IG_DISPLAY_BASE\n    var ps = Number(Q.prosperity_soylent)\n    if (!isNaN(ps)) {\n        Q.stock_soylent = Math.round(142.9 * ps / scale * 10) / 10\n    }\n    var pm = Number(Q.prosperity_magrail)\n    if (!isNaN(pm)) {\n        Q.stock_magrail = Math.round(98.8 * pm / scale * 10) / 10\n    }\n    var pb = Number(Q.prosperity_brazos)\n    if (!isNaN(pb)) {\n        Q.stock_brazos = Math.round(201.3 * pb / scale * 10) / 10\n    }\n    var pf = Number(Q.prosperity_families)\n    if (!isNaN(pf)) {\n        Q.cloud_9_street_price = Math.round(34.3 * pf / scale * 10) / 10\n    }\n    var pe = Number(Q.prosperity_edges)\n    if (!isNaN(pe)) {\n        Q.edges_cyber_insurance_premium = Math.round(280.7 * pe / scale * 10) / 10\n    }\n    var pk = Number(Q.prosperity_sharks)\n    if (!isNaN(pk)) {\n        Q.ocular_index = Math.round(89.6 * pk / scale)\n    }\n}\n\n// might want to put some spending inertia into this system later on\n\n\nfunction organize_tick(){\n    Q.protest_good += Q.non_violence -1.5 // hurt corp popularity with all every time it reaches 10\n    Q.protest_bad += 2.25 - Q.non_violence // corp gain popularity with drones and affluent and lose with poor every time it reaches 10\n}\n\nfunction trade_level_tick(){\n    if (Q.phase == 1) { Q.trade_level *= 1.0016667 }\n    if (Q.phase == 2) { Q.trade_level *= 0.9994444 }\n    if (Q.phase == 3) { Q.trade_level *= 0.9983333 }\n    if (Q.phase == 4) { Q.trade_level *= 0.9994444 }\n\n}\n\n// Tourism would also cover high end bespoke services like ship detailing\nfunction tourism_revenue() {\n    Q.overall_tourism = 2345 * Q.trade_level / (Q.crime_level * Q.crime_level)\n\n    Q.corp_tourism_rev = Q.overall_tourism * .41 + Q.overall_tourism * Q.trade_level\n    Q.affluent_tourism_rev = Q.overall_tourism * .39 - Q.overall_tourism * Q.trade_level\n    Q.coop_tourism_rev =  (Q.overall_tourism - Q.corp_tourism - Q.affluent_tourism)*.43\n    Q.poor_tourism_rev =  (Q.overall_tourism - Q.corp_tourism - Q.affluent_tourism)*.57\n}\n\nfunction longshore_revenue() {\n    Q.overall_longshore = 2678 * Q.trade_level / (Q.crime_level * Q.crime_level)\n\n    Q.coop_longshore_rev = Q.overall_longshore * .38 - Q.overall_longshore * Q.trade_level\n    Q.affluent_longshore_rev = Q.overall_longshore * .41 - Q.overall_longshore * Q.trade_level\n    Q.corp_longshore_rev =  (Q.overall_longshore - Q.corp_longshore - Q.affluent_longshore)*.31\n    Q.poor_longshore_rev =  (Q.overall_longshore - Q.corp_longshore - Q.affluent_longshore)*.33\n    Q.drone_longshore_rev =  (Q.overall_longshore - Q.corp_longshore - Q.affluent_longshore)*.36\n}\n\n// would be nice if there was a way to make drones shift to buying local as they get more independent minded\nfunction corp_purchase(){\n    corp_spend = Q.corp_money / 84\n    Q.corp_local_spend = corp_spend / Q.trade_level * .2\n    drone_spend = Q.drone_money * 0.60\n    Q.drone_local_spend = drone_spend / Q.trade_level * .4\n\n    Q.corp_capital_change = Q.corp_capital*.01 + (corp_spend *0.8 * Math.sqrt(Q.trade_level))\n\n    Q.corp_capital_revenue = corp_capital / 240\n}\n\nfunction coop_purchase(){\n    coop_spend = Q.coop_money / 36\n    Q.coop_spend\n\n}\n\n\nfunction economy_tick(){\n    trade_level_tick()\n    corp_purchase()\n}\n\n// Economic asset API: economic_assets_runtime.scene.dry\n\n\nfunction monthly_tick_phase1() {\n    organize_tick()\n    trade_level_tick()\n    economy_tick()\n    applyStratumProsperityBumps()\n    if (typeof Q.applyEconomicAssetProsperity === 'function') {\n        Q.applyEconomicAssetProsperity()\n    }\n    applyProsperityConnections()\n    if (typeof Q.processMonthlyHousing === 'function') {\n        Q.processMonthlyHousing()\n    }\n    syncProsperityDisplays()\n}\n\n/** Add amount to every voter block in one district; sync prosperity displays. */\nfunction bumpDistrictProsperity(districtId, amount) {\n    var state = Q.electionV2\n    if (!state || !state.blocks) { return false }\n    var bump = Number(amount)\n    if (isNaN(bump) || bump === 0) { return false }\n    for (var i = 0; i < state.blocks.length; i++) {\n        var block = state.blocks[i]\n        if (block.district.id !== districtId) { continue }\n        block.prosperity = clampProsperity(\n            (block.prosperity ?? PROSPERITY_BLOCK_BASELINE) + bump\n        )\n    }\n    if (typeof Q.qified_block_prosperity === 'function') {\n        Q.qified_block_prosperity()\n    }\n    if (typeof Q.syncStratumProsperityAverages === 'function') {\n        Q.syncStratumProsperityAverages()\n    }\n    return true\n}\n\nQ.monthly_tick_phase1 = monthly_tick_phase1\nQ.applyStratumProsperityBumps = applyStratumProsperityBumps\nQ.bumpDistrictProsperity = bumpDistrictProsperity\nQ.applyProsperityConnections = applyProsperityConnections\nQ.syncProsperityDisplays = syncProsperityDisplays\n\ninitEconomyProsperity()\nif (typeof Q.initEconomicAssets === 'function') { Q.initEconomicAssets() }\nsyncProsperityDisplays()\n\nif (typeof Q.syncIgAttitudes === 'function') { Q.syncIgAttitudes() }\n\n!}"

ElectorateModifier('_ref_Deeps_I', 'district', 'Deeps', 'I', 0.4),\n// new ElectorateModifier('_ref_Deeps_H', 'district', 'Deeps', 'H', -0.3),\n\n/** How radical the xenophobia is (0–1 linear, then sqrt above 1). Higher → harder for Dems to claw back. */\nfunction effectiveParochialRadicalism(rad) {\n    if (isNaN(rad) || rad <= 0) { return 0 }\n    if (rad <= 1) { return rad }\n    return Math.sqrt(rad)\n}\n\n/** Share of derived xenophobia Dems can claw off H (1 − radicalism, floored at 0). */\nfunction demClawBackFraction(rad) {\n    return Math.max(0, 1 - effectiveParochialRadicalism(rad))\n}\n\n/**\n * Derive modifier stacks from Q.parochialism (0–100).\n * xenophobia_derived: ramps above Suspicion (41+). H+/C− on 1st/2nd via catalog.\n * xenophobia_clawed_back: derived * demClawBackFraction(rad); C still hit by derived only.\n */\nQ.syncParochialismXenophobiaStacks = function(q) {\n    q = q || Q\n    var p = Number(q.parochialism)\n    if (isNaN(p)) { p = 0 }\n    var rad = Number(q.parochial_radicalism)\n    if (isNaN(rad)) { rad = 0.65 }\n    var clawMult = demClawBackFraction(rad)\n\n    var suspicionFloor = 41\n    var maxStack = 20\n    var raw = p <= suspicionFloor ? 0 : ((p - suspicionFloor) / (100 - suspicionFloor)) * maxStack\n\n    q.xenophobia_derived = raw\n    q.xenophobia_clawed_back = raw * clawMult\n}\n\nQ.applyElectorateModifiers = function() {\n    var state = Q.electionV2\n    if (!state) { return }\n\n    Q.syncParochialismXenophobiaStacks(Q)\n\n    state.demographicsById = state.demographicsById || Object.fromEntries(\n        state.demographics.map(function(d) { return [d.id, d] })\n    )\n    state.districtsById = state.districtsById || Object.fromEntries(\n        state.districts.map(function(d) { return [d.id, d] })\n    )\n\n    // Districts are not rebuilt by syncAllVoteAppeals; reset from baseline each poll.\n    for (var j = 0; j < state.districts.length; j++) {\n        var dist = state.districts[j]\n        dist.competitiveScores = electorateCopyScores(\n            dist._baseCompetitiveScores || dist.competitiveScores\n        )\n    }\n    // Strata/global already base+appeals from election runtime sync; add stack deltas only.\n\n    for (var m = 0; m < Q.electorateModifierCatalog.length; m++) {\n        Q.electorateModifierCatalog[m].apply(state, Q)\n    }\n}\n// MIKASA_ELECTORATE_RUNTIME_END\n\nelectorate_runtime_ready = 1\n\n!}\ngo-to: jumpScene\n"
}

## [2026-06-06T20:42:07Z] assistant (afterAgentResponse)

Updated `contracting.scene.dry` with district subchoices, new per-choice flags, prosperity bumps, and electorate modifiers.

## Structure

Top-level picks → district submenus for Big Three, wealthy, and coop paths:

| Path | District options | Stack flag |
|------|------------------|------------|
| **Outside corps** | *(none)* | `contract_outside` *(unchanged)* |
| **Big Three** | Docks / Vats / Railyard / Limelight | `contract_big3_docks`, `_vats`, `_railyard`, `_limelight` |
| **Local wealthy** | Docks / Aurora | `contract_wealthy_docks`, `contract_wealthy_aurora` |
| **Coops & guilds** | Aurora / Pitts / Deeps / Docks | `contract_coop_aurora`, `_pitts`, `_deeps`, `_docks` |

Removed: `contract_big3`, `contract_petty`, `contract_coop`, `contract_wealthy`.

## Mechanics per path

- **Outside corps** — `contract_outside`; poll **D** +0.08, **C** +0.1 (rebellion favors later).
- **Big Three — Docks** — all three `*_reprisal` −1; Docks prosperity +0.06; Docks **D**, drone **C**, global **C** −.
- **Big Three — home district** — favored corp `*_leverage` +1; other two `*_reprisal` +1; district prosperity +0.08; district **C**/**D**, drone **C**.
- **Wealthy** — `rich_bump` +0.05 (citywide); district prosperity +0.05; rich **C**/**I**.
- **Coops** — district prosperity bump; district **D**; coop/deeper **D** where relevant; global **G** +0.05–0.08; Pitts also `families_leverage` +1.

## Infrastructure

- Added `Q.bumpDistrictProsperity(districtId, amount)` in `organize_phase_tick.scene.dry`.
- Bootstrapped via `setup_functions` so it’s available before cards are played.
- Replaced old contract modifiers in `electorate_runtime.scene.dry` with rows for all new stack flags.

`lint:dendry` and `make-html` both pass.

## [2026-06-06T20:46:04Z] assistant (afterAgentResponse)

**Not really** — there’s no `tabulateBudget()` / `updateBudget()` that rolls up all income and spending each month.

What exists today:

### `Q.budget` (a plain quality)
- Shown on **Status** as `Budget: [+ budget +]`
- **Not initialized** in `root.scene.dry` (starts at Dendry’s default `0` unless something sets it)
- **Manual bumps** in events, e.g. Dundalk affair: `budget += 2` per branch

### Closest thing to tabulation: housing tax
In `economic_assets_runtime.scene.dry`:

- **`applyHousingTaxRevenue()`** — loops operational homes in `Q.economicAssets`, sums  
  `0.1 × district_multiplier × owner_prosperity` per home (Aurora homes count 5× vs Docks/Pitts), stores result in `Q.monthly_housing_tax`, then **adds it to `Q.budget`**
- Called from **`processMonthlyHousing()`** at month-end housing processing

There’s **no** similar rollup for asset taxes, tourism, fines, etc. — those are only sketched in `revenues.tbd`.

### What doesn’t run yet
`processMonthlyHousing()` is only invoked from `monthly_tick_phase1()` in `organize_phase_tick.scene.dry`, and **that tick isn’t wired to Advance / `post_event`**, so housing tax → budget likely **doesn’t fire** during normal play unless something else calls it.

### Electorate hook
`budget` has a catalog row in `electorate_runtime.scene.dry` with **delta 0** (tracking only, no poll effect).

---

**Summary:** budget is a counter you increment by hand or via `applyHousingTaxRevenue()`; there’s no full municipal budget engine yet. If you want one, the natural place would be something like `Q.tabulateBudget()` in `economic_assets_runtime` (housing tax + asset tax from `revenues.tbd` + event deltas), called from the monthly tick. I can wire that up if you want.

|| 0\n\n    if (total > maxTotal) {\n        var trim = roundHousingUnits(total - maxTotal)\n        unoccupied = roundHousingUnits(Math.max(0, unoccupied - trim))\n        total = maxTotal\n <= 0) { return 0 }\n\n    var slot = ensureBucketHousingTotals(bucket)\n    var listed = housingBucketListedEconomic(slot)\n    var total = slot.total || 0\n    var unoccupied = slot.unoccupied || 0\n      var block = state.blocks[b]\n        var entry = byBlockId[block.id]\n        if (!entry || entry.destroyed <= 0) { continue }\n        var sid = block.demographic.id\n        var ownedCitywide = block.prosperity = clampEconomicBlockProsperity(\n            (block.prosperity ?? 6) + penalty\n        )\n        changed = true\n    }\n    if (changed && typeof Q.qified_block_prosperity === { id: 'public_safety', label: 'Police and public safety payroll', amount: 0 },\n]\n\nfunction sumBudgetRecurringExpenses() {\n    var catalog = Q.budgetRecurringExpenses || BUDGET_RECURRING_EXPENSES\n Q.budgetExpenseQueue.push({\n        amount: amount,\n        source: sourceId || 'misc',\n        label: label || sourceId || 'misc',\n        month: Q.month,\n        year: Q.year,\n    })\n    return true\n}\n\nfunction computeHousingTaxRevenue() {\n    ensureEconomicAssets()\n    ensureHousingInventory()\n    var revenue = 0\n    var assets = Q.economicAssets || []\n\n    for (var i = 0; i var expenses = recurring + queued\n    var net = revenue - expenses\n\n    Q.monthly_housing_tax = revenue\n    Q.monthly_budget_revenue = revenue\n    Q.monthly_budget_recurring_expenses = PROSPERITY_INTRA_DISTRICT_DEFAULT\n    var perDistrict = Q.prosperityIntraDistrictMesh || null\n\n    for (var d = 0; d < districts.length; d++) {\n        var districtId = districts[d]\n        var matrix = (perDistrict && perDistrict[districtId])\n            ? perDistrict[districtId]\n            : defaultMesh\n        var blocks = prosperityBlocksInDistrict(state, districtId)\n        if (blocks.length < 2) { continue }\n\n        for (var si = 0; si < blocks.length; si++) {\n            var source = blocks[si]\n            var fromSid = source.demographic.id\n            var signal = source.prosperity ?? PROSPERITY_BLOCK_BASELINE\n            for (var ti = 0; ti < blocks.length; ti++) {\n                var target = blocks[ti]\n                if (source.id === target.id) { continue }\n                var coeff = prosperityIntraMeshCoeff(\n                    matrix,\n                    fromSid,\n                    target.demographic.id\n                )\n                if (coeff === 0) { continue }\n                var weight = prosperityTransferMultiplier(\n                    source.population,\n                    target.population\n                )\n                var transfer = coeff * signal * weight\n                if (transfer === 0) { continue }\n                blockDeltas[target.id] = (blockDeltas[target.id] || 0) + transfer\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nfunction applyIgHomeDistrictProsperityLinks(state, q, blockDeltas) {\n    var links = Q.prosperityIgHomeDistrictLinks || PROSPERITY_IG_HOME_DISTRICT_LINKS\n    for (var li = 0; li < links.length; li++) {\n        var row = links[li]\n        var igKey = 'prosperity_' + String(row.ig).toLowerCase()\n        var igVal = q[igKey]\n        if (igVal == null) { igVal = igProsperityDefault(row.ig) }\n        var signal = Number(igVal)\n        if (isNaN(signal)) { signal = igProsperityDefault(row.ig) }\n        var sourcePop = PROSPERITY_IG_EFFECTIVE_POP[row.ig]\n        if (sourcePop == null) { sourcePop = 20 }\n        var blocks = prosperityBlocksInDistrict(state, row.district)\n        var toStrata = row.toStrata || {}\n\n        for (var bi = 0; bi < blocks.length; bi++) {\n            var block = blocks[bi]\n            var sid = block.demographic.id\n            if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(toStrata, sid)) {\n                continue\n            }\n            var coeff = Number(toStrata[sid])\n            if (isNaN(coeff) || coeff === 0) { continue }\n            var weight = prosperityTransferMultiplier(sourcePop, block.population)\n            var transfer = coeff * signal * weight\n            if (transfer === 0) { continue }\n            blockDeltas[block.id] = (blockDeltas[block.id] || 0) + transfer\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nfunction applyDocksTradeProsperityLinks(state, q, blockDeltas) {\n    var spec = Q.prosperityDocksTrade || PROSPERITY_DOCKS_TRADE\n    var excess = economyQualityExcess(q, spec.quality)\n    if (excess === 0) { return }\n    var sourcePop = prosperityDistrictTotalPop(state, spec.district)\n    if (sourcePop <= 0) { return }\n    var blocks = prosperityBlocksInDistrict(state, spec.district)\n    var strataCoeffs = spec.strata || {}\n\n    for (var bi = 0; bi < blocks.length; bi++) {\n        var block = blocks[bi]\n        var sid = block.demographic.id\n        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(strataCoeffs, sid)) {\n            continue\n        }\n        var strataMult = Number(strataCoeffs[sid])\n        if (isNaN(strataMult) || strataMult === 0) { continue }\n        var coeff = Number(spec.coefficient) * strataMult\n        if (isNaN(coeff) || coeff === 0) { continue }\n        var weight = prosperityTransferMultiplier(sourcePop, block.population)\n        var transfer = coeff * excess * weight\n        if (transfer === 0) { continue }\n        blockDeltas[block.id] = (blockDeltas[block.id] || 0) + transfer\n    }\n}\n\nfunction applyPittsCrimeProsperityLinks(state, q, blockDeltas) {\n    var spec = Q.prosperityPittsCrime || PROSPERITY_PITTS_CRIME\n    var excess = economyQualityExcess(q, spec.quality)\n    if (excess === 0) { return }\n    var sourcePop = prosperityDistrictTotalPop(state, spec.district)\n    if (sourcePop <= 0) { return }\n    var blocks = prosperityBlocksInDistrict(state, spec.district)\n    var strataCoeffs = spec.strata || {}\n    var baseCoeff = Number(spec.coefficient)\n    if (isNaN(baseCoeff)) { baseCoeff = 0 }\n\n    for (var bi = 0; bi < blocks.length; bi++) {\n        var block = blocks[bi]\n        var sid = block.demographic.id\n        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(strataCoeffs, sid)) { continue }\n        var strataMult = Number(strataCoeffs[sid])\n        if (isNaN(strataMult) || strataMult === 0) { continue }\n        var coeff = baseCoeff * strataMult\n        var weight = prosperityTransferMultiplier(sourcePop, block.population)\n        var transfer = coeff * excess * weight\n        if (transfer === 0) { continue }\n        blockDeltas[block.id] = (blockDeltas[block.id] || 0) + transfer\n    }\n}\n\n/**\n * One-shot citywide prosperity grants per stratum via Q.<stratum>_bump (e.g. coop_bump).\n * Adds bump to every voter block in that class, clamps, syncs Q, then sets the key to 0.\n * Cards:  on-arrival: coop_bump = 0.05\n * Same month: call Q.applyStratumProsperityBumps() in on-arrival after setting bump.\n * Otherwise applied at the start of monthly_tick_phase1 (before connection bleed).\n */\nfunction applyStratumProsperityBumps() {\n    var state = Q.electionV2\n    if (!state || !state.blocks) { return false }\n\n    var strata = Q.stratum_labels || [\n        'rich', 'first', 'second', 'coop', 'drone', 'alien', 'deeper', 'third_wave',\n    ]\n    var changed = false\n\n    for (var s = 0; s < strata.length; s++) {\n        var sid = strata[s]\n        var key = sid + '_bump'\n        var raw = Q[key]\n        if (raw == null || raw === 0) {\n            Q[key] = 0\n            continue\n        }\n        var bump = Number(raw)\n        Q[key] = 0\n        if (isNaN(bump) || bump === 0) { continue }\n\n        for (var i = 0; i < state.blocks.length; i++) {\n            var block = state.blocks[i]\n            if (block.demographic.id !== sid) { continue }\n            block.prosperity = clampProsperity(\n                (block.prosperity ?? PROSPERITY_BLOCK_BASELINE) + bump\n            )\n        }\n        changed = true\n    }\n\n    if (changed && typeof Q.qified_block_prosperity === 'function') {\n        Q.qified_block_prosperity()\n    }\n    return changed\n}\n\nfunction applyProsperityConnections() {\n    var state = Q.electionV2\n    if (!state || !state.blocks || !Q.prosperityConnectionCatalog) { return }\n\n    initEconomyProsperity()\n\n    var blockDeltas = {}\n    var igDeltas = {}\n    var catalog = Q.prosperityConnectionCatalog\n\n    for (var i = 0; i < catalog.length; i++) {\n        catalog[i].accumulate(state, Q, blockDeltas, igDeltas)\n    }\n\n    applyDistrictIntraProsperityMesh(state, blockDeltas)\n    applyIgHomeDistrictProsperityLinks(state, Q, blockDeltas)\n    applyDocksTradeProsperityLinks(state, Q, blockDeltas)\n    applyPittsCrimeProsperityLinks(state, Q, blockDeltas)\n\n    for (var b = 0; b < state.blocks.length; b++) {\n        var block = state.blocks[b]\n        var delta = blockDeltas[block.id]\n        if (delta) {\n            block.prosperity = clampProsperity(\n                (block.prosperity ?? Q.coop_spend\n\n}\n\n\nfunction economy_tick(){\n    trade_level_tick()\n    corp_purchase()\n}\n\n// Economic asset API: economic_assets_runtime.scene.dry\n\n\nfunction monthly_tick_phase1() {\n @type {Record<string, number>} */\n    const FIRST_WAVE_WEIGHTS = {\n        japanese: 50,\n        french: 13,\n        german: 13,\n        italian: 12,\n        english: 12,\n    }\n\n    function catalogKind(isHome) {\n        return isHome ? 'homes' : 'assets'\n    }\n\n    function hashSeed(seed) {\n        var s = String(seed == null ? '' : seed)\n        var h = 0\n        for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) {\n            h = ((h << 5) - h) + s.charCodeAt(i)\n            h |= 0\n        }\n        return Math.abs(h)\n    }\n\n    function pick(pool, seed) {\n        if (!pool || !pool.length) { return '' }\n        var idx = hashSeed(seed) % pool.length\n        return pool[idx]\n    }\n\n    function pickWeighted(buckets, seed) {\n        var total = 0\n        for (var i = 0; i < buckets.length; i++) {\n            total += buckets[i].weight\n        }\n        if (total <= 0) { return pick(buckets[0] && buckets[0].pool, seed) }\n        var roll = hashSeed(seed) % total\n        for (var j = 0; j < buckets.length; j++) {\n            roll -= buckets[j].weight\n            if (roll < 0) {\n                return pick(buckets[j].pool, seed + ':' + j)\n            }\n        }\n        return pick(buckets[buckets.length - 1].pool, seed)\n    }\n\n    function pickFirstWave(seed, isHome) {\n        var wave = CATALOG[catalogKind(isHome)].first_wave\n        var buckets = []\n        for (var key in FIRST_WAVE_WEIGHTS) {\n            if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(FIRST_WAVE_WEIGHTS, key)) { continue }\n            buckets.push({\n                weight: FIRST_WAVE_WEIGHTS[key],\n                pool: wave[key],\n            })\n        }\n        return pickWeighted(buckets, 'first:' + seed)\n    }\n\n    function pickSecondWave(seed, isHome) {\n        var wave = CATALOG[catalogKind(isHome)].second_wave\n        return pickWeighted([\n            { weight: 50, pool: wave.swahili },\n            { weight: 50, pool: wave.south_asian },\n        ], 'second:' + seed)\n    }\n\n    function pickCoop(seed, isHome) {\n        if (hashSeed('coop-branch:' + seed) % 2 === 0) {\n            return pickFirstWave('coop:' + seed, isHome)\n        }\n        return pickSecondWave('coop:' + seed, isHome)\n    }\n\n    function pickBrazos(seed, isHome) {\n        var b = CATALOG[catalogKind(isHome)].brazos\n        return pickWeighted([\n            { weight: 45, pool: b.tejano },\n            { weight: 55, pool: b.corporate },\n        ], 'brazos:' + seed)\n    }\n\n    function pickSoylent(seed, isHome) {\n        return pick(CATALOG[catalogKind(isHome)].soylent, 'soylent:' + seed)\n    }\n\n    function pickMagrail(seed, isHome) {\n        return pick(CATALOG[catalogKind(isHome)].magrail, 'magrail:' + seed)\n    }\n\n    function pickDeeper(seed, isHome) {\n        var pool = CATALOG[catalogKind(isHome)].deeper\n        var name = pick(pool, 'deeper:' + seed)\n        if (!name && !isHome) {\n            return pick(CATALOG.homes.deeper, 'deeper:' + seed)\n        }\n        return name\n    }\n\n    function pickThirdWave(seed, isHome) {\n        var kind = catalogKind(isHome)\n        var fw = CATALOG[kind].first_wave\n        var sw = CATALOG[kind].second_wave\n        return pickWeighted([\n            { weight: 28, pool: fw.japanese },\n            { weight: 8, pool: fw.french },\n            { weight: 8, pool: fw.german },\n            { weight: 7, pool: fw.italian },\n            { weight: 7, pool: fw.english },\n            { weight: 22, pool: sw.swahili },\n            { weight: 15, pool: sw.south_asian },\n            { weight: 5, pool: CATALOG[kind].magrail },\n        ], 'third:' + seed)\n    }\n\n    function pickRich(seed, isHome) {\n        var kind = catalogKind(isHome)\n        return pickWeighted([\n            { weight: 30, pool: CATALOG[kind].soylent },\n            { weight: 30, pool: CATALOG[kind].brazos.corporate },\n            { weight: 25, pool: CATALOG[kind].magrail },\n            { weight: 15, pool: CATALOG[kind].brazos.tejano },\n        ], 'rich:' + seed)\n    }\n\n    function pickOwnerDisplayName(opts) {\n        opts = opts || {}\n        var owner = String(opts.owner || '')\n        var isHome = !!opts.isHome\n        var seed = opts.id || (owner + '_' + (opts.district || '') + '_' + (isHome ? 'home' : 'asset'))\n\n        if (opts.ownerType === 'ig') {\n            if (owner === 'Soylent') { return pickSoylent(seed, isHome) }\n            if (owner === 'Magrail') { return pickMagrail(seed, isHome) }\n            if (owner === 'Brazos') { return pickBrazos(seed, isHome) }\n            return pick(owner.toLowerCase(), seed) || owner + ' Facility'\n        }\n\n        if (owner === 'first') { return pickFirstWave(seed, isHome) }\n        if (owner === 'second') { return pickSecondWave(seed, isHome) }\n        if (owner === 'third_wave') { return pickThirdWave(seed, isHome) }\n        if (owner === 'rich') { return pickRich(seed, isHome) }\n        if (owner === 'deeper') { return pickDeeper(seed, isHome) }\n        if (owner === 'coop') { return pickCoop(seed, isHome) }\n\n        return pickThirdWave(seed, isHome)\n    }\n\n    q.pickOwnerDisplayName = pickOwnerDisplayName\n}\n// END economic-name-catalog\n\n\nfunction ensureEconomicAssets() {\n    if (!Q.economicAssets) { Q.economicAssets = [] }\n}\n\n/** Monthly prosperity bump per site (homes do not contribute). */\nvar ECONOMIC_PROSPERITY_HOME_DELTA = 0\nvar ECONOMIC_PROSPERITY_ASSET_DELTA = 0.1\nvar ECONOMIC_PROSPERITY_BLOCK_CLAMP_MIN = 0\nvar ECONOMIC_PROSPERITY_BLOCK_CLAMP_MAX = 20\n\n/** Home turf per corp / syndicate (election weights + prosperity connection districts). */\nvar IG_HOME_DISTRICT = {\n    Soylent: 'Vats',\n    Magrail: 'Railyard',\n    Brazos: 'Limelight',\n    Families: 'Pitts',\n    Edges: 'Railyard',\n    Sharks: 'Deeps',\n}\n\nvar STARTER_ECONOMIC_ASSET_BATCHES = [\n    { ownerType: 'ig', owner: 'Brazos', district: IG_HOME_DISTRICT.Brazos, count: 25 },\n    { ownerType: 'ig', owner: 'Magrail', district: IG_HOME_DISTRICT.Magrail, count: 27 },\n    { ownerType: 'ig', owner: 'Soylent', district: IG_HOME_DISTRICT.Soylent, count: 28 },\n    { ownerType: 'ig', owner: 'Families', district: IG_HOME_DISTRICT.Families, count: 9 },\n    { ownerType: 'ig', owner: 'Edges', district: IG_HOME_DISTRICT.Edges, count: 7 },\n    { ownerType: 'ig', owner: 'Sharks', district: IG_HOME_DISTRICT.Sharks, count: 7 },\n    { ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'coop', district: 'Aurora', count: 20 },\n    { ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'rich', district: 'Docks', count: 6 },\n    { ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'first', district: 'Aurora', count: 5 },\n    { ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'second', district: 'Aurora', count: 3 },\n    { ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'alien', district: 'Aurora', count: 1 },\n]\n\nfunction clampEconomicBlockProsperity(value) {\n    var lo = Q.prosperityClampMin != null\n        ? Q.prosperityClampMin\n        : ECONOMIC_PROSPERITY_BLOCK_CLAMP_MIN\n    var hi = Q.prosperityClampMax != null\n        ? Q.prosperityClampMax\n        : ECONOMIC_PROSPERITY_BLOCK_CLAMP_MAX\n    return Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, value))\n}\n\nfunction economicProsperityDeltaForEntry(entry) {\n    if (entry.prosperityDelta != null) {\n        return Number(entry.prosperityDelta)\n    }\n    return entry.countsAsHome\n        ? ECONOMIC_PROSPERITY_HOME_DELTA\n        : ECONOMIC_PROSPERITY_ASSET_DELTA\n}\n\nfunction addEconomicRecord(rec) {\n    ensureEconomicAssets()\n    var entry = {\n        level: 1,\n        health: 1,\n        major: false,\n        infraWeight: 0.8,\n        outputPerLevel: 0.08,\n        countsAsHome: false,\n    }\n    for (var k in rec) {\n        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rec, k)) { entry[k] = rec[k] }\n    }\n    if (entry.countsAsHome) {\n        entry.kind = entry.kind || 'megaplex'\n        entry.outputPerLevel = 0\n        if (!entry.infraWeight || entry.infraWeight === 0.8) { entry.infraWeight = 1.2 }\n    } else if (!entry.kind) {\n        entry.kind = 'office'\n    }\n    if (entry.prosperityDelta == null) {\n        entry.prosperityDelta = entry.countsAsHome\n            ? ECONOMIC_PROSPERITY_HOME_DELTA\n            : ECONOMIC_PROSPERITY_ASSET_DELTA\n    }\n    if (!entry.id) {\n        entry.id = entry.owner + '_' + entry.district + '_' + Q.economicAssets.length\n    }\n    Q.economicAssets.push(entry)\n    return entry\n}\n\nfunction addAsset(opts) {\n    var rec = {}\n    for (var k in opts) {\n        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(opts, k)) { rec[k] = opts[k] }\n    }\n    rec.countsAsHome = false\n    return addEconomicRecord(rec)\n}\n\nfunction addHome(opts) {\n    var rec = {}\n    for (var k in opts) {\n        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(opts, k)) { rec[k] = opts[k] }\n    }\n    rec.countsAsHome = true\n    return addEconomicRecord(rec)\n}\n\n/** Strata that own housing stock (not drones / corps). Pop in 10,000s; 1 home per 100k people. */\nvar ECONOMIC_HOME_STRATA = [\n    'rich', 'first', 'second', 'coop', 'third_wave', 'alien', 'deeper',\n]\n\n/** Minimum working homes for population (pop in 10,000s → 1 home per 100k people). */\nfunction homesRequiredForPopulation(popInTenThousands) {\n    var pop = Number(popInTenThousands)\n    if (isNaN(pop) || pop <= 0) { return 0 }\n    return Math.ceil(pop / 10)\n}\n\n/** Max owned homes (listed + unoccupied counter) = ceil(need × 1.1). */\nfunction homesBuildCapForNeed(needUnits) {\n    var n = Number(needUnits)\n    if (isNaN(n) || n <= 0) { return 0 }\n    return Math.ceil(n * 1.1)\n}\n\n/** Listed occupied cap; surplus becomes unoccupied (not in asset lists). */\nfunction homesOccupiedCapForNeed(needUnits) {\n    var n = Number(needUnits)\n    if (isNaN(n) || n <= 0) { return 0 }\n    return Math.floor(n)\n}\n\nvar HOUSING_SQM_PER_HOME = 10000\nvar HOUSING_TAX_BASE_PER_HOME = 0.1\n/** District share of 0.1 budget/home (Aurora 50% → 0.05/home at prosperity 1). */\nvar HOUSING_TAX_DISTRICT_MULTIPLIER = {\n    Docks: 0.1,\n    Pitts: 0.1,\n    Aurora: 0.5,\n}\n\n/** Deficit blocks filled in district order; phase-3 lenders use this stratum order. */\nvar HOUSING_BORROW_DISTRICT_ORDER = [\n    'Docks', 'Aurora', 'Vats', 'Railyard', 'Pitts', 'Limelight', 'Deeps',\n]\nvar HOUSING_BORROW_STRATUM_PRIORITY = [\n    'rich', 'coop', 'first', 'second', 'deeper', 'drone', 'third_wave', 'alien',\n]\n\n/** Big Three dormitory stock for drone VoterBlocks (not stratum-owned housing). */\nvar CORP_DRONE_HOUSING_IGS = ['Soylent', 'Magrail', 'Brazos']\n\nfunction isEconomicHomeOperational(entry) {\n    if (!entry || !entry.countsAsHome) { return false }\n    if (entry.health == null) { return true }\n    return Number(entry.health) >= 1\n}\n\nfunction economicHomeServesStratum(entry, stratumId) {\n    if (!entry || !entry.countsAsHome) { return false }\n    if (isCorpDroneHousing(entry)) { return false }\n    if (entry.ownerType === 'stratum' && entry.owner === stratumId) {\n        return true\n    }\n    if (entry.ownerType === 'public' && entry.housingFor === stratumId) {\n        return true\n    }\n    return false\n}\n\nfunction isCorpDroneHousing(entry) {\n    return !!(\n        entry\n        && entry.countsAsHome\n        && entry.ownerType === 'ig'\n        && entry.housingFor === 'drone'\n        && CORP_DRONE_HOUSING_IGS.indexOf(entry.owner) >= 0\n    )\n}\n\nfunction countCorpDroneHousingInDistrict(assets, corp, districtId) {\n    var operational = 0\n    var destroyed = 0\n    for (var i = 0; i < assets.length; i++) {\n        var e = assets[i]\n        if (!isCorpDroneHousing(e)) { continue }\n        if (e.owner !== corp || e.district !== districtId) { continue }\n        if (isEconomicHomeOperational(e)) {\n            operational += 1\n        } else {\n            destroyed += 1\n        }\n    }\n    return { operational: operational, destroyed: destroyed }\n}\n\nfunction nextCorpDroneHomeId(corp, district) {\n    var i = 0\n    while (hasEconomicAssetId('corp_drone_' + corp + '_' + district + '_' + i)) {\n        i += 1\n    }\n    return 'corp_drone_' + corp + '_' + district + '_' + i\n}\n\nfunction addCorpDroneHome(corp, district, id) {\n    var name = corp + ' dormitory'\n    if (typeof Q.pickOwnerDisplayName === 'function') {\n        name = Q.pickOwnerDisplayName({\n            ownerType: 'ig',\n            owner: corp,\n            district: district,\n            id: id,\n            isHome: true,\n        })\n    }\n    return addHome({\n        id: id,\n        name: name,\n        district: district,\n        ownerType: 'ig',\n        owner: corp,\n        housingFor: 'drone',\n        major: false,\n    })\n}\n\nfunction ensureCorpDroneHomesInDistrict(corp, district, targetOperational) {\n    ensureEconomicAssets()\n    var housing = countCorpDroneHousingInDistrict(Q.economicAssets, corp, district)\n    var toPlace = targetOperational - housing.operational\n    if (toPlace <= 0) { return 0 }\n    var placed = 0\n    for (var n = 0; n < toPlace; n++) {\n        var homeId = nextCorpDroneHomeId(corp, district)\n        addCorpDroneHome(corp, district, homeId)\n        placed += 1\n    }\n    return placed\n}\n\nfunction buildDroneDistrictPopulations(state) {\n    var byDistrict = {}\n    if (!state || !state.blocks) { return byDistrict }\n    for (var bi = 0; bi < state.blocks.length; bi++) {\n        var block = state.blocks[bi]\n        if (block.demographic.id !== 'drone') { continue }\n        var did = block.district.id\n        byDistrict[did] = (byDistrict[did] || 0) + block.population\n    }\n    return byDistrict\n}\n\n/** Big Three dormitories only in each corp's home district (drone pop basis). */\nfunction seedCorpDroneHousing(state) {\n    if (Q.corpDroneHousingSeeded) { return }\n    if (!state || !state.blocks || typeof Q.pickOwnerDisplayName !== 'function') {\n        return\n    }\n    ensureEconomicAssets()\n    purgeCorpDroneHomesOutsideHomeDistricts()\n    var droneByDistrict = buildDroneDistrictPopulations(state)\n\n    for (var ci = 0; ci < CORP_DRONE_HOUSING_IGS.length; ci++) {\n        var corp = CORP_DRONE_HOUSING_IGS[ci]\n        var homeDist = IG_HOME_DISTRICT[corp]\n        var homeNeed = homesOccupiedCapForNeed(\n            homesRequiredForPopulation(droneByDistrict[homeDist] || 0)\n        )\n        ensureCorpDroneHomesInDistrict(corp, homeDist, homeNeed)\n    }\n\n    Q.corpDroneHousingSeeded = 1\n}\n\nfunction purgeCorpDroneHomesOutsideHomeDistricts() {\n    ensureEconomicAssets()\n    var kept = []\n    for (var i = 0; i < Q.economicAssets.length; i++) {\n        var e = Q.economicAssets[i]\n        if (!isCorpDroneHousing(e)) {\n            kept.push(e)\n            continue\n        }\n        if (e.district === IG_HOME_DISTRICT[e.owner]) {\n            kept.push(e)\n        }\n    }\n    Q.economicAssets = kept\n}\n\nfunction corpDronePoolLendRoom(pool) {\n    return Math.max(0, pool.operational - pool.lent)\n}\n\nfunction buildCorpDroneHousingPools(assets) {\n    var pools = []\n    for (var ci = 0; ci < CORP_DRONE_HOUSING_IGS.length; ci++) {\n        var corp = CORP_DRONE_HOUSING_IGS[ci]\n        for (var di = 0; di < HOUSING_BORROW_DISTRICT_ORDER.length; di++) {\n            var dist = HOUSING_BORROW_DISTRICT_ORDER[di]\n            var h = countCorpDroneHousingInDistrict(assets, corp, dist)\n            if (h.operational <= 0) { continue }\n            pools.push({\n                corp: corp,\n                district: dist,\n                operational: h.operational,\n                lent: 0,\n            })\n        }\n    }\n    return pools\n}\n\n/** Corps lend all operational drone dorm surplus in-district before block-to-block borrow. */\nfunction lendCorpDroneHousingFirst(entries) {\n    ensureEconomicAssets()\n    var assets = Q.economicAssets || []\n    var pools = buildCorpDroneHousingPools(assets)\n    if (!pools.length || !entries.length) { return }\n\n    var droneBorrowers = []\n    for (var i = 0; i < entries.length; i++) {\n        var entry = entries[i]\n        if (entry.stratum !== 'drone') { continue }\n        if (blockHousingDeficit(entry) <= 0) { continue }\n        droneBorrowers.push(entry)\n    }\n    if (!droneBorrowers.length) { return }\n\n    droneBorrowers.sort(function(a, b) {\n        var da = housingBorrowDistrictSortKey(a.district)\n        var db = housingBorrowDistrictSortKey(b.district)\n        if (da !== db) { return da - db }\n        return 0\n    })\n\n    for (var bi = 0; bi < droneBorrowers.length; bi++) {\n        var borrower = droneBorrowers[bi]\n        var need = blockHousingDeficit(borrower)\n        for (var pi = 0; pi < pools.length && need > 0; pi++) {\n            var pool = pools[pi]\n            if (pool.district !== borrower.district) { continue }\n            var room = corpDronePoolLendRoom(pool)\n            if (room <= 0) { continue }\n            var n = Math.min(need, room)\n            pool.lent += n\n            borrower.borrowed += n\n            need -= n\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nfunction countStratumHousing(assets, stratumId) {\n    var total = 0\n    var operational = 0\n    var destroyed = 0\n    for (var i = 0; i < assets.length; i++) {\n        var e = assets[i]\n        if (!economicHomeServesStratum(e, stratumId)) { continue }\n        total += 1\n        if (isEconomicHomeOperational(e)) {\n            operational += 1\n        } else {\n            destroyed += 1\n        }\n    }\n    return { total: total, operational: operational, destroyed: destroyed }\n}\n\nfunction ensureHousingInventory() {\n    if (!Q.economicHousingInventory) { Q.economicHousingInventory = {} }\n}\n\nfunction housingInventoryKey(ownerType, owner, district) {\n    return ownerType + ':' + owner + ':' + district\n}\n\nfunction getHousingInventorySlot(key) {\n    ensureHousingInventory()\n    if (!Q.economicHousingInventory[key]) {\n        Q.economicHousingInventory[key] = { total: 0, unoccupied: 0 }\n    }\n    return Q.economicHousingInventory[key]\n}\n\n/** Housing stock counts (listed economic + slack) to three decimal places. */\nfunction roundHousingUnits(value) {\n    var n = Number(value)\n    if (isNaN(n)) { return 0 }\n    return Math.round(n * 1000) / 1000\n}\n\nfunction normalizeHousingSlot(slot) {\n    if (!slot) { return }\n    slot.unoccupied = roundHousingUnits(slot.unoccupied || 0)\n    slot.total = roundHousingUnits(slot.total || 0)\n    if (slot.total < slot.unoccupied) {\n        slot.unoccupied = slot.total\n    }\n}\n\nfunction roundAllHousingInventory() {\n    ensureHousingInventory()\n    var inv = Q.economicHousingInventory || {}\n    for (var key in inv) {\n        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(inv, key)) { continue }\n        normalizeHousingSlot(inv[key])\n    }\n}\n\nfunction housingBucketListedEconomic(slot) {\n    if (!slot) { return 0 }\n    return roundHousingUnits(Math.max(0, (slot.total || 0) - (slot.unoccupied || 0)))\n}\n\nfunction ensureBucketHousingTotals(bucket) {\n    var key = housingInventoryKey(bucket.ownerType, bucket.owner, bucket.district)\n    var slot = getHousingInventorySlot(key)\n    var regListed = countListedHomesForBucket(bucket)\n    if (slot.total == null || isNaN(Number(slot.total))) {\n        slot.total = roundHousingUnits(regListed + (slot.unoccupied || 0))\n    } else if (slot.total + 0.0005 < regListed + (slot.unoccupied || 0)) {\n        slot.total = roundHousingUnits(\n            Math.max(slot.total, regListed + (slot.unoccupied || 0))\n        )\n    }\n    normalizeHousingSlot(slot)\n    return slot\n}\n\n/** Add registry sites when economic listed (fractional) crosses the next whole home. */\nfunction syncListedRegistryHomesForBucket(bucket, slot) {\n    var economicListed = housingBucketListedEconomic(slot)\n    var reg = countListedHomesForBucket(bucket)\n    var need = bucket.required + bucket.lent\n    var occupiedCap = homesOccupiedCapForNeed(need)\n    while (reg < Math.ceil(economicListed - 1e-9) && reg < occupiedCap) {\n        addListedHomeForBucket(bucket)\n        reg += 1\n    }\n}\n\n/** Initial slack = occupied homes × 2 × prosperity × 1% (three decimal places). */\nfunction initialHousingSlackUnits(listedHomes, addListedHomeForBucket(bucket)\n        reg += 1\n    }\n}\n\n/** Initial slack = occupied homes × 2 × prosperity × 1% (three decimal places). */\nfunction initialHousingSlackUnits(listedHomes, var igKey = 'prosperity_' + String(owner).toLowerCase()\n        var v = Q[igKey]\n        if (v == null || isNaN(Number(v))) { return 6 }\n        return Number(v)\n    }\n    var sk = 'prosperity_' }\n    return countStratumHousingInDistrict(\n        Q.economicAssets,\n        bucket.owner,\n        bucket.district\n    ).operational\n}\n\nfunction countStratumHousingInDistrict(assets, }\n        if (!economicHomeServesStratum(e, stratumId)) { continue }\n        if (isEconomicHomeOperational(e)) {\n            operational += 1\n        } else {\n            destroyed += 1\n        }\n    }\n    return { operational: operational, destroyed: destroyed }\n}\n\nfunction removeOneListedHomeFromBucket(bucket) {\n    ensureEconomicAssets()\n    for (var i = Q.economicAssets.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n        var e = Q.economicAssets[i]\n        if (e.district !== bucket.district) { continue }\n        if (bucket.ownerType === 'ig') {\n            if (!isCorpDroneHousing(e) || e.owner !== bucket.owner) { continue }\n        } else if (!economicHomeServesStratum(e, bucket.owner)) {\n            continue\n        }\n        Q.economicAssets.splice(i, 1)\n        return true\n    }\n    return false\n}\n\nfunction addListedHomeForBucket(bucket) {\n    if (typeof Q.pickOwnerDisplayName !== 'function') { return }\n    if (bucket.ownerType === 'ig') {\n        var id = || 0\n\n    if (total > maxTotal) {\n        var trim = roundHousingUnits(total - maxTotal)\n        unoccupied = roundHousingUnits(Math.max(0, unoccupied - trim))\n        total = maxTotal\n        slot.total = total\n        slot.unoccupied = unoccupied\n        listed = housingBucketListedEconomic(slot)\n    }\n\n    if (listed > occupiedCap) {\n        var shift = roundHousingUnits(listed - housingBucketListedEconomic(slot)\n        unoccupied = slot.unoccupied\n        total = slot.total\n    }\n\n    var reg = countListedHomesForBucket(bucket)\n    while (reg > Math.ceil(housingBucketListedEconomic(slot) + 1e-9) && reg > 0) {\n        if (removeOneListedHomeFromBucket(bucket)) {\n            reg -= 1\n        } else {\n            break\n        }\n    }\n    syncListedRegistryHomesForBucket(bucket, slot)\n    normalizeHousingSlot(slot)\n    return {\n        listed: housingBucketListedEconomic(slot),\n        unoccupied: slot.unoccupied,\n        need: need,\n        maxTotal: maxTotal,\n    }\n}\n\nfunction buildHomesForBucket(bucket) {\n    var need = bucket.required + bucket.lent\n    var occupiedCap = homesOccupiedCapForNeed(need)\n    var maxTotal = homesBuildCapForNeed(need)\n    var prosperity = ownerProsperityForHousing(bucket.ownerType, bucket.owner)\n    var toBuild = monthlyHomesBuildRate(prosperity, bucket.pop)\n    if (toBuild <= 0) { return 0 }\n\n    var slot = ensureBucketHousingTotals(bucket)\n    var listed = housingBucketListedEconomic(slot)\n    var total = slot.total || 0\n    var unoccupied = slot.unoccupied || 0\n    var room = roundHousingUnits(maxTotal - total)\n    if (room <= 0) { return 0 }\n\n    var add = roundHousingUnits(Math.min(toBuild, room))\n    if (add <= 0) { return 0 }\n\n    var needListed = Math.max(0, occupiedCap - listed)\n    var toListed = roundHousingUnits(Math.min(add, needListed))\n    var toUnocc = roundHousingUnits(add - toListed)\n\n    slot.total = roundHousingUnits(total + countCitywideHomesForStratum(stratumId) {\n    ensureEconomicAssets()\n    ensureHousingInventory()\n    var total = 0\n    var prefix = 'stratum:' + stratumId + ':'\n    var inv = Q.economicHousingInventory || {}\n    var hasSlot = {}\n    for (var slotKey in inv) {\n        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(inv, slotKey)) { continue }\n        if (slotKey.indexOf(prefix) !== 0) { continue }\n        hasSlot[slotKey] = true\n        var slot = inv[slotKey]\n        if (slot.total != null && !isNaN(Number(slot.total))) {\n            total += e.district)\n        if (hasSlot[key]) { continue }\n        if (isEconomicHomeOperational(e)) {\n            total += 1\n        }\n    }\n    return roundHousingUnits(total)\n}\n\n/** Per destroyed home: prosperity −= 1 / citywide homes owned by that stratum. */\nfunction applyDestroyedHomeProsperity(state, ledger) {\n    if (!state || !state.blocks || !ledger) { return false }\n    var byBlockId = {}\n    for (var i = 0; i < ledger.length; i++) {\n        byBlockId[ledger[i].blockId] = ledger[i]\n    }\n    var changed = false\n    for (var b = 0; b < state.blocks.length; b++) {\n        var block = state.blocks[b]\n        var entry = byBlockId[block.id]\n        if (!entry || entry.destroyed <= 0) { continue }\n        var sid = block.demographic.id\n        var ownedCitywide = countCitywideHomesForStratum(sid)\n        if (ownedCitywide <= 0) { ownedCitywide = 1 }\n        var perHome = 1 / ownedCitywide\n        var penalty = -perHome * entry.destroyed\n        block.prosperity = clampEconomicBlockProsperity(\n            (block.prosperity ?? 6) + penalty\n        )\n        changed = true\n    }\n    if (changed && typeof Q.qified_block_prosperity === 'function') {\n        Q.qified_block_prosperity()\n    }\n    return changed\n}\n\n/** Recurring monthly municipal debits (template — raise amounts as policies go live). */\nvar BUDGET_RECURRING_EXPENSES = [\n    { id: 'baseline_admin', label: 'Municipal administration', amount: 0 },\n    { id: 'grid_maintenance', label: 'Grid and life-support maintenance', amount: 0 },\n    { id: 'public_safety', label: 'Police and public safety payroll', amount: 0 },\n]\n\nfunction sumBudgetRecurringExpenses() {\n    var catalog = Q.budgetRecurringExpenses || BUDGET_RECURRING_EXPENSES\n    var total = 0\n    for (var i = 0; i < catalog.length; i++) {\n        var row = catalog[i]\n        var amt = Number(row.amount)\n        if (!isNaN(amt) && amt > 0) { total += amt }\n    }\n    return total\n}\n\nfunction sumBudgetQueuedExpenses() {\n    var queue = Q.budgetExpenseQueue || []\n    var total = 0\n    for (var j = 0; j < queue.length; j++) {\n        var amt = Number(queue[j].amount)\n        if (!isNaN(amt) && amt > 0) { total += amt }\n    }\n    return total\n}\n\n/** One-shot costs (cards, events) applied on the next Q.tabulateBudget(). */\nfunction queueBudgetExpense(amount, sourceId, label) {\n    amount = Number(amount)\n    if (isNaN(amount) || amount <= 0) { return false }\n    if (!Q.budgetExpenseQueue) { Q.budgetExpenseQueue = [] }\n    Q.budgetExpenseQueue.push({\n        amount: amount,\n        source: sourceId || 'misc',\n        label: label || sourceId || 'misc',\n        month: Q.month,\n        year: Q.year,\n    })\n    return true\n}\n\nfunction computeHousingTaxRevenue() {\n    ensureEconomicAssets()\n    ensureHousingInventory()\n    var revenue = 0\n    var assets = Q.economicAssets || []\n\n    for (var i = 0; i < assets.length; i++) {\n        var e = assets[i]\n        if (!e.countsAsHome || !isEconomicHomeOperational(e)) { continue }\n        var district = e.district\n        var mult = HOUSING_TAX_DISTRICT_MULTIPLIER[district]\n        if (mult == null || mult <= 0) { continue }\n        var ownerPros = ownerProsperityForHousing(\n            e.ownerType,\n            e.owner,\n            e.housingFor\n        )\n        revenue += HOUSING_TAX_BASE_PER_HOME * mult * ownerPros\n    }\n\n    return Math.round(revenue * 1000) / 1000\n}\n\n/**\n * Monthly budget rollup: housing tax revenue minus recurring + queued expenses.\n * Event bonuses (e.g. dundalk_reform budget += 2) stay as direct Q.budget edits.\n */\nfunction tabulateBudget(opts) {\n    opts = opts || {}\n    ensureEconomicAssets()\n    ensureHousingInventory()\n\n    var revenue = computeHousingTaxRevenue()\n    var recurring = sumBudgetRecurringExpenses()\n    var queued = sumBudgetQueuedExpenses()\n    var expenses = recurring + queued\n    var net = revenue - expenses\n\n    Q.monthly_housing_tax = revenue\n    Q.monthly_budget_revenue = revenue\n    Q.monthly_budget_recurring_expenses = Math.round(recurring * 1000) / 1000\n    Q.monthly_budget_queued_expenses = Math.round(queued * 1000) / 1000\n    Q.monthly_budget_expenses = Math.round(expenses * 1000) / 1000\n    Q.monthly_budget_net = Math.round(net * 1000) / 1000\n\n    if (opts.apply !== false) {\n        Q.budget = Math.round(((Number(Q.budget) || 0) + net) * 1000) / 1000\n        Q.budgetExpenseQueue = []\n    }\n\n    return { revenue: revenue, expenses: expenses, net: net }\n}\n\n/**\n * Borrow surplus, destroyed-home prosperity hit, prosperity-scaled builds,\n * unoccupied counters. ensureHousingInventory()\n\n    var ledger = redistributeBlockHousingSurplus(state)\n    if (!ledger) { return false }\n\n    var changed = applyDestroyedHomeProsperity(state, ledger)\n\n    var state)\n    for (var ck in corpBuckets) {\n        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(corpBuckets, ck)) { continue }\n        var cb = corpBuckets[ck]\n        if (buildHomesForBucket(cb) > 0)  for (var igKey in igDeltas) {\n        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(igDeltas, igKey)) { continue }\n        var igVal = Q[igKey]\n        if (igVal == null) { igVal = 1 }\n        Q[igKey] = igVal + igDeltas[igKey]\n        changed = true\n    }\n\n    if (changed && typeof Q.qified_block_prosperity === 'function') {\n        Q.qified_block_prosperity()\n    }\n    return changed\n}\n\n/** Seed homes once at start — 120% of need; population still recovering from its nadir. */\nfunction initEconomicAssets() {\n    if (typeof installEconomicNameCatalog === 'function' &&   first: '1st Wave',\n    second: '2nd Wave',\n    coop: 'Coops',\n    drone: 'Drones',\n    alien: 'Otherworlders',\n    deeper: 'Deepers',\n    third_wave: '3rd Wave',\n}\n\nfunction {\n    var listed = Number(homeCount) || 0\n    var unocc = Number(unoccupiedCount) || 0\n    var total = listed + unocc\n    if (total <= 0) { return 0 }\n    return Math.round((1000 * unocc) / s.replace(/\\.?0+$/, '') + '%'\n}\n\nfunction enrichEconomicReportGroup(row) {\n    row.sqm10k = housingSqm10kUnits(\n        row.homes,\n        row.ownerType,\n        row.owner,\n        row.housingFor\n    )\n    row.slackPct = slackCapacityPercent(row.homes, row.unoccupied)\n    row.slackLabel = formatSlackCapacityPercent(row.slackPct)\n    return row\n}\n\n/** Fractional listed               label: label,\n                ownerType: ownerType,\n                owner: owner,\n                housingFor: housingFor || null,\n                homes: 0,\n                unoccupied: 0,\n                assets: 0,\n            }\n            order.push(key)\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (var i = 0; i < assets.length; i++) {\n        var e = assets[i]\n        var key = var unocc = slot.unoccupied || 0\n        if (listed <= 0 && unocc <= 0) { continue }\n        var parts = slotKey.split(':')\n        if (parts.length < 3) { continue }\n        var ownerType = parts[0]\n        var owner = parts[1]\n        var mock = { ownerType: ownerType, owner: owner }\n        if (ownerType === 'ig') { mock.housingFor = 'drone' }\n        var key = economicAffiliationGroupKey(mock)\n        ensureBucket(\n            key,\n            affiliationTextPlain(mock),\n            ownerType,\n            owner,\n            mock.housingFor\n )\n    }\n\n    for (var hi = 0; hi < assets.length; hi++) {\n        var home = assets[hi]\n        if (!home.countsAsHome) { continue }\n        var hkey = economicAffiliationGroupKey(home)\n        if (buckets[hkey] && buckets[hkey].homes > 0) { continue }\n        ensureBucket(\n            hkey,\n            affiliationTextPlain(home),\n            home.ownerType,\n            home.owner,\n            home.housingFor\n        )\n        buckets[hkey].homes += 1\n    }\n\n    var rows = []\n    for (var j = 0; j < order.length; j++) {\n        rows.push(enrichEconomicReportGroup(buckets[order[j]]))\n    }\n    rows.sort(function(a, b) {\n        var ta = a.sqm10k + a.assets\n        var tb = b.sqm10k + b.assets\n        if (tb !== ta) { return tb - ta }\n        return a.label < b.label ? -1 : (a.label > b.label ? 1 : 0)\n    })\n    return rows\n}\n\nfunction economicAssetsReportTotals(groupRows) {\n    var totalSqm10k = 0\n    var totalOwned = 0\n    var totalUnoccupied = 0\n    var totalAssets = 0\n    for (var i = 0; i < groupRows.length; i++) {\n        var g = groupRows[i]\n        totalSqm10k += g.sqm10k || 0\n        totalOwned += g.homes + (g.unoccupied || 0)\n        totalUnoccupied += g.unoccupied || 0\n        totalAssets += g.assets\n    }\n    var slackPct = slackCapacityPercent(\n        totalOwned - totalUnoccupied,\n        totalUnoccupied\n    )\n    return {\n        sqm10k: Math.round(totalSqm10k * 10) / 10,\n        unoccupied: totalUnoccupied,\n        slackPct: slackPct,\n        slackLabel: formatSlackCapacityPercent(slackPct),\n        assets: totalAssets,\n        sites: groupRows.reduce(function(n, g) {\n            return n + g.homes + g.assets\n        }, 0),\n    }\n}\n\n/** Plain-text holdings summary (see scheduled_elections election_report pattern). */\nfunction formatEconomicAssetsReportText() {\n    if (typeof ensureEconomicAssets !== 'function') {\n        return 'Economic registry is not available yet.'\n    }\n    var groups = aggregateEconomicAssetsByGroup()\n    if (!groups.length) {\n        return 'No registered homes or economic assets yet.'\n    }\n\n    var labelWidth = 10\n    for (var gi = 0; gi < groups.length; gi++) {\n        if (groups[gi].label.length > labelWidth) {\n            labelWidth = groups[gi].label.length\n        }\n    }\n\n    var lines = []\n    lines.push(\n        padEconomicReportField('Demographic', labelWidth)\n        + '  '\n        + padEconomicReportField('Occupied', 8)\n        + '  '\n        + padEconomicReportField('Residential', 8)\n        + '  Assets'\n    )\n    lines.push(\n        padEconomicReportField('', labelWidth)\n        + '  '\n        + padEconomicReportField('m² (×10k)', 8)\n        + '  '\n        + padEconomicReportField('Slack cap %', 8)\n        + '  '\n    )\n    for (var i = 0; i < groups.length; i++) {\n        var g = groups[i]\n        lines.push(\n            padEconomicReportField(g.label, labelWidth)\n            + '  '\n            + padEconomicReportField(String(g.sqm10k), 8)\n            + '  '\n            + padEconomicReportField(g.slackLabel, 8)\n            + '  '\n            + String(g.assets)\n        )\n    }\n    var totals = economicAssetsReportTotals(groups)\n    lines.push('')\n    lines.push(\n        totals.sqm10k\n        + ' ×10k m² owned (occupied stock), '\n        + totals.slackLabel\n        + ' slack capacity, '\n        + totals.assets\n        + ' assets ('\n        + totals.sites\n        + ' listed sites).'\n    )\n    return lines.join('\\n')\n}\n\nfunction padEconomicReportField(text, width) {\n    var s = String(text)\n    while (s.length < width) { s += ' ' }\n    return s\n}\n\nfunction   "content": "title: Contracts and supplies\nnew-page: true\nis-card: true\ncard-image: img/asteroid.jpg\ntags: local_organizing, city_affairs\nview_if: organizing_unlock = 1\n\nWho gets the next round of Aurora procurement contracts — and where the work actually happens — shapes both neighborhood prosperity and who owes you favors later.\n\n- @outside_corporations\n- @big_3\n- @local_wealthy\n- @cooperatives\n\n@outside_corporations\ntitle: Outside corporation suppliers\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_outside += 1;\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') {\n    Q.queueBudgetExpense(2, 'contract_award', 'Outside supplier contracts');\n}\n!}\n\nWe steer contracts toward offstation suppliers with no permanent Mikasa footprint.  The Big 3 are a little annoyed but would be much angrier if they realized this lets us cultivate outside relationships that could be used against them one day.\n\n@big_3\ntitle: Big Three suppliers\n\nCommunity Soylent, Magrail Combine, and Brazos Zaibatsu all want the paperwork routed their way.  We can split the award across the neutral Docks hub — calming everyone a little — or concentrate work in one corp's home district and make a real friend there.\n\n- @big_3_docks: Split contracts through the Docks hub\n- @big_3_vats: Concentrate in the Vats (Community Soylent)\n- @big_3_railyard: Concentrate in the Railyard (Magrail Combine)\n- @big_3_limelight: Concentrate in the Limelight (Brazos Zaibatsu)\n\n@big_3_docks\ntitle: Split contracts through the Docks hub\nsubtitle: Spread the work; lower antagonism with all three megacorps\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_big3_docks = 1;\nsoylent_reprisal = Math.max(0, (Number(Q.soylent_reprisal) || 0) - 1);\nmagrail_reprisal = Math.max(0, (Number(Q.magrail_reprisal) || 0) - 1);\nbrazos_reprisal = Math.max(0, (Number(Q.brazos_reprisal) || 0) - 1);\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'docks_big3_contract_hub', name: 'Docks trilateral contract hub', district: 'Docks', ownerType: 'public', owner: 'city', kind: 'office', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Docks', 0.06); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\nif (typeof Q.syncIgAttitudes === 'function') { Q.syncIgAttitudes(); }\n!}\n\nLongshore clerks in the Docks process a three-way split.  None of the megacorps are thrilled, but none can claim we played favorites.  Trade clerks and corporate drones both see a little more steady work.\n\n@big_3_vats\ntitle: Concentrate in the Vats\nsubtitle: Favor Community Soylent; antagonize Magrail and Brazos\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_big3_vats = 1;\nQ.soylent_leverage = (Number(Q.soylent_leverage) || 0) + 1;\nQ.magrail_reprisal = (Number(Q.magrail_reprisal) || 0) + 1;\nQ.brazos_reprisal = (Number(Q.brazos_reprisal) || 0) + 1;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'vats_soylent_contract_yard', name: 'Soylent municipal contract yard', district: 'Vats', ownerType: 'ig', owner: 'Soylent', kind: 'factory', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Vats', 0.08); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\nif (typeof Q.syncIgAttitudes === 'function') { Q.syncIgAttitudes(); }\n!}\n\nSoylent compound managers take victory laps while rival corp liaisons file angry protests.  Vats employment ticks up; drone households tied to Soylent notice the difference first.\n\n@big_3_railyard\ntitle: Concentrate in the Railyard\nsubtitle: Favor Magrail Combine; antagonize Soylent and Brazos\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_big3_railyard = 1;\nQ.magrail_leverage = (Number(Q.magrail_leverage) || 0) + 1;\nQ.soylent_reprisal = (Number(Q.soylent_reprisal) || 0) + 1;\nQ.brazos_reprisal = (Number(Q.brazos_reprisal) || 0) + 1;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'railyard_magrail_contract_depot', name: 'Magrail municipal contract depot', district: 'Railyard', ownerType: 'ig', owner: 'Magrail', kind: 'factory', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Railyard', 0.08); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\nif (typeof Q.syncIgAttitudes === 'function') { Q.syncIgAttitudes(); }\n!}\n\nMagrail foremen expand shift schedules; Soylent and Brazos quietly route their complaints through syndicate channels.  The Railyard hums with freight-adjacent contract labor.\n\n@big_3_limelight\ntitle: Concentrate in the Limelight\nsubtitle: Favor Brazos Zaibatsu; antagonize Soylent and Magrail\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_big3_limelight = 1;\nQ.brazos_leverage = (Number(Q.brazos_leverage) || 0) + 1;\nQ.soylent_reprisal = (Number(Q.soylent_reprisal) || 0) + 1;\nQ.magrail_reprisal = (Number(Q.magrail_reprisal) || 0) + 1;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'limelight_brazos_contract_studio', name: 'Brazos municipal contract studio', district: 'Limelight', ownerType: 'ig', owner: 'Brazos', kind: 'factory', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Limelight', 0.08); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\nif (typeof Q.syncIgAttitudes === 'function') { Q.syncIgAttitudes(); }\n!}\n\nBrazos showcase halls pick up maintenance and fit-out work.  The other two megacorps treat the award as a deliberate snub — useful leverage if we meant to pick a side.\n\n@local_wealthy\ntitle: Local wealthy contractors\n\nShareholder-adjacent workshops and Docks rentiers both bid for the same municipal line items.  Either cluster works; the city's moneyed strata gossip about the award no matter which district hosts the signing ceremony.\n\n- @wealthy_docks: Award through Docks firms\n- @wealthy_aurora: Award through Aurora firms\n\n@wealthy_docks\ntitle: Award through Docks firms\nsubtitle: Prosperity in the Docks; shareholders citywide take notice\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_wealthy_docks = 1;\nQ.rich_bump = 0.05;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'docks_wealthy_contract_office', name: 'Docks shareholder contract office', district: 'Docks', ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'rich', kind: 'office', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.applyStratumProsperityBumps === 'function') { Q.applyStratumProsperityBumps(); }\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Docks', 0.05); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\n!}\n\nDocks shareholders pocket the prime subcontracts while their cousins in other districts still benefit from the spending ripple.  The old money crowd approves of government that pays on time.\n\n@wealthy_aurora\ntitle: Award through Aurora firms\nsubtitle: Prosperity in Aurora; shareholders citywide take notice\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_wealthy_aurora = 1;\nQ.rich_bump = 0.05;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'aurora_wealthy_contract_office', name: 'Aurora boutique contract office', district: 'Aurora', ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'rich', kind: 'office', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.applyStratumProsperityBumps === 'function') { Q.applyStratumProsperityBumps(); }\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Aurora', 0.05); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\n!}\n\nAurora's boutique fabricators and logistics brokers land the contracts.  Wealthy households across Mikasa still feel the downstream orders — and remember who signed the checks.\n\n@cooperatives\ntitle: Local cooperatives and guilds\n\nCooperative workshops and guild ateliers can absorb small-batch municipal orders if we place the work where they already live.  Every site gives the local guilds a little more clout citywide.\n\n- @coop_aurora: Aurora cooperatives\n- @coop_pitts: Pitts cooperatives and Seven Families workshops\n- @coop_deeps: Deeps cooperatives\n- @coop_docks: Docks cooperatives and longshore guilds\n\n@coop_aurora\ntitle: Aurora cooperatives\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_coop_aurora = 1;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'aurora_coop_contract_workshop', name: 'Aurora coop contract workshop', district: 'Aurora', ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'coop', kind: 'workshop', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Aurora', 0.06); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\n!}\n\nAurora coop yards pick up fabrication and life-support maintenance orders.  Guild brokers in other districts note that municipal business is no longer reserved for megacorp vendors.\n\n@coop_pitts\ntitle: Pitts cooperatives and guild workshops\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_coop_pitts = 1;\nQ.families_leverage = (Number(Q.families_leverage) || 0) + 1;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'pitts_coop_contract_workshop', name: 'Pitts coop guild workshop', district: 'Pitts', ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'coop', kind: 'workshop', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Pitts', 0.07); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\nif (typeof Q.syncIgAttitudes === 'function') { Q.syncIgAttitudes(); }\n!}\n\nPitts cooperative machine shops and syndicate-tolerant guild floors share the workload.  The Seven Families treat the contract as recognition; coop members treat it as proof the city can hire locally.\n\n@coop_deeps\ntitle: Deeps cooperatives\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_coop_deeps = 1;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'deeps_coop_contract_workshop', name: 'Deeps sealed contract workshop', district: 'Deeps', ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'coop', kind: 'workshop', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Deeps', 0.06); }\n!}\n\nDeepers' insulated workshops take contracts on their own terms — sealed bids, air-gapped ledgers, no corp middleware. We can split the award across the neutral Docks hub — calming everyone a little — or concentrate work in one corp's home district and make a real friend there.\n\n- @big_3_docks: Split contracts through the Docks hub\n- @big_3_vats: Concentrate in the Vats (Community Soylent)\n- @big_3_railyard: Concentrate in the Railyard (Magrail Combine)\n- @big_3_limelight: Concentrate in the Limelight (Brazos Zaibatsu)\n\n@big_3_docks\ntitle: Split contracts through the Docks hub\nsubtitle: Spread the work; lower antagonism with all three megacorps\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_big3_docks = 1;\nsoylent_reprisal = Math.max(0, (Number(Q.soylent_reprisal) || 0) - 1);\nmagrail_reprisal = Math.max(0, (Number(Q.magrail_reprisal) || 0) - 1);\nbrazos_reprisal = Math.max(0, (Number(Q.brazos_reprisal) || 0) - 1);\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'docks_big3_contract_hub', name: 'Docks trilateral contract hub', district: 'Docks', ownerType: 'public', owner: 'city', kind: 'office', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Docks', 0.06); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\nif (typeof Q.syncIgAttitudes === 'function') { Q.syncIgAttitudes(); }\n!}\n\nLongshore clerks in the Docks process a three-way split.  None of the megacorps are thrilled, but none can claim we played favorites. Vats employment ticks up; drone households tied to Soylent notice the difference first.\n\n@big_3_railyard\ntitle: Concentrate in the Railyard\nsubtitle: Favor Magrail Combine; antagonize Soylent and Brazos\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_big3_railyard = 1;\nQ.magrail_leverage = (Number(Q.magrail_leverage) || 0) + 1;\nQ.soylent_reprisal = (Number(Q.soylent_reprisal) || 0) + 1;\nQ.brazos_reprisal = (Number(Q.brazos_reprisal) || 0) + 1;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'railyard_magrail_contract_depot', name: 'Magrail municipal contract depot', district: 'Railyard', ownerType: 'ig', owner: 'Magrail', kind: 'factory', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Railyard', 0.08); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\nif (typeof Q.syncIgAttitudes === 'function') { Q.syncIgAttitudes(); }\n!}\n\nMagrail foremen expand shift schedules; Soylent and Brazos quietly route their complaints through syndicate channels.  The Railyard hums with freight-adjacent contract labor.\n\n@big_3_limelight\ntitle: Concentrate in the Limelight\nsubtitle: Favor Brazos Zaibatsu; antagonize Soylent and Magrail\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_big3_limelight = 1;\nQ.brazos_leverage = (Number(Q.brazos_leverage) || 0) + 1;\nQ.soylent_reprisal = (Number(Q.soylent_reprisal) || 0) + 1;\nQ.magrail_reprisal = (Number(Q.magrail_reprisal) || 0) + 1;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'limelight_brazos_contract_studio', name: 'Brazos municipal contract studio', district: 'Limelight', ownerType: 'ig', owner: 'Brazos', kind: 'factory', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Limelight', 0.08); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\nif (typeof Q.syncIgAttitudes === 'function') { Q.syncIgAttitudes(); }\n!}\n\nBrazos showcase halls pick up maintenance and fit-out work.  The other two megacorps treat the award as a deliberate snub — useful leverage if we meant to pick a side.\n\n@local_wealthy\ntitle: Local wealthy contractors\n\nShareholder-adjacent workshops and Docks rentiers both bid for the same municipal line items.
