Every site gives the local guilds a little more clout citywide.\n\n- @coop_aurora: Aurora cooperatives\n- @coop_pitts: Pitts cooperatives and Seven Families workshops\n- @coop_deeps: Deeps cooperatives\n- @coop_docks: Docks cooperatives and longshore guilds\n\n@coop_aurora\ntitle: Aurora cooperatives\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_coop_aurora = 1;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'aurora_coop_contract_workshop', name: 'Aurora coop contract workshop', district: 'Aurora', ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'coop', kind: 'workshop', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Aurora', 0.06); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\n!}\n\nAurora coop yards pick up fabrication and life-support maintenance orders. The Seven Families treat the contract as recognition; coop members treat it as proof the city can hire locally.\n\n@coop_deeps\ntitle: Deeps cooperatives\non-arrival: {!\ncontract_coop_deeps = 1;\nif (typeof Q.addAsset === 'function') {\n    Q.addAsset({ id: 'deeps_coop_contract_workshop', name: 'Deeps sealed contract workshop', district: 'Deeps', ownerType: 'stratum', owner: 'coop', kind: 'workshop', major: false });\n}\nif (typeof Q.bumpDistrictProsperity === 'function') { Q.bumpDistrictProsperity('Deeps', 0.06); }\nif (typeof Q.queueBudgetExpense === 'function') { Q.queueBudgetExpense(3, 'contract_award', 'Municipal contract award'); }\n!}\n\nDeepers' insulated workshops take contracts on their own terms — sealed bids, air-gapped ledgers, no corp middleware. ## [2026-06-06T20:48:27Z] assistant (afterAgentResponse)

Budget tabulation is wired up. Here's what was added:

## `Q.tabulateBudget()` (`economic_assets_runtime.scene.dry`)

Each month it computes:

| | Source |
|---|--------|
| **Revenue** | `computeHousingTaxRevenue()` — housing tax from operational homes |
| **Recurring expenses** | `Q.budgetRecurringExpenses` template (amounts `0` for now) |
| **Queued expenses** | `Q.budgetExpenseQueue` — one-shots from cards/events |

Then: `Q.budget += revenue − expenses`, and writes breakdown qualities:
- `monthly_housing_tax`, `monthly_budget_revenue`, `monthly_budget_recurring_expenses`, `monthly_budget_queued_expenses`, `monthly_budget_expenses`, `monthly_budget_net`

## Expense template

```javascript
Q.budgetRecurringExpenses = [
  { id: 'baseline_admin', label: 'Municipal administration', amount: 0 },
  { id: 'grid_maintenance', label: 'Grid and life-support maintenance', amount: 0 },
  { id: 'public_safety', label: 'Police and public safety payroll', amount: 0 },
]
```

Raise `amount` on any row when those policies go live.

## `Q.queueBudgetExpense(amount, sourceId, label)`

Queues a one-shot debit applied on the **next** `tabulateBudget()` (month advance), then clears the queue.

## Monthly wiring

- **`post_event`** calls `Q.monthly_tick_phase1()` (housing sim + prosperity + **`tabulateBudget()`**)
- Direct event bonuses like Dundalk `budget += 2` still apply immediately, outside the monthly rollup

## Contracting costs

- **Outside corps:** `queueBudgetExpense(2, …)`
- **All district branches:** `queueBudgetExpense(3, …)`

Cost hits when you **advance the month**, not when you pick the card (same month’s tabulation applies the queue).

## [2026-06-06T20:51:37Z] assistant_thought (afterAgentThought)

The contract scene will be limited to two views per phase. Unused visits will carry over.

I need to find how Dendry tracks card visits and phases. The limit likely restricts how many times a card can appear per phase.

Unused visits from one phase roll over into the next. A leftover slot plus two fresh ones gives three total uses across phases. I will implement this using card qualities.

A `contracting_visits_remaining` counter will track the budget. It resets to two at each phase start and accumulates unused slots from previous phases.

The card’s `view-if` will check `contracting_visits_remaining` and `organizing_unlock`. Each visit decrements the remaining count. A `contracting_allowance_through_phase` quality will track which phase already received its two free visits, so the carryover logic does not double-grant.

The allowance will be granted via a monthly tick or visibility check whenever the current phase exceeds the last granted phase. Unused slots roll over by adding two per phase gap. The card must also receive its initial two free visits when it first becomes available during the organize phase.

The card’s `view-if` will require both `organizing_unlock` and `contracting_visits_remaining > 0`. JavaScript cannot safely run inside Dendry’s `view-if` expressions, so the phase allowance will be synced through a `post_event` tick instead.

The allowance sync will be a standalone `syncContractingAllowance` function invoked from `post_event` or `organize_phase_tick`. It reads the current phase and the last granted phase, then adds two free visits for every phase gap between them. This handles cases where the phase counter has not yet incremented.

The sync function will default the last granted phase to zero and add two visits for every phase gap. The remaining count will be initialized on first grant. The card’s `view-if` will require `organizing_unlock` and at least one remaining visit. The sync will run in `post_event` before the main tick and again on `organize_phase_start` so the budget is ready before the card is checked.
